פֶּ֥סַח ה֖וּא לַיהוָֽה · pesach hu la-YHWH · 'it is a passover to the LORD'

DERIVATION RECORD — freeze-era draft note: DERIVED 2026-08-09 · FORWARD ERA unit #26 (run FWD-6 block 2; Exod 1-12 continuous behind it). Span: 12:1-51, whole chapter (the corpus's largest single unit: 51 verses, SNAPSHOT-verified; three verses without etnachta — 12:1, 12:45, 12:47 — split at their strongest disjunctive per the canon). Oral layer IN-PIPELINE: manifest 16/16 VERIFIED, zero FAILED — see oral_audit_note_en. MACHINE PROFILE. SEVEN DECLAREs, THREE RESULTs, TWO EVENTS — the statute-book's first audit. The PERPETUAL-STATUTE class DEBUTS: four cards pushed with no in-Torah pop, OPEN by class forever — the calendar (12:2, the Torah's first commandment), the memorial-feast (12:14), the matza-week (12:15), the answer-script (12:26, the Haggada's charter receipting 10:2's story-clause). Two command-decks receipted IN-UNIT by the compliance-formula: the lamb-command (12:3) and Moses' relay (12:21) both pop at 12:28 — where the verse carries TWO do-verbs (asserted; the Kitzur: 'two doings — pesach and mila'); the pesach-ordinance (12:43) pops at 12:50. TWO EVENTS: the STRIKE (12:29 — makat bekhorot, by the Name alone, at the half of the night; the forecast-grid's slave-girl→CAPTIVE swap asserted; 4:23's oldest arm receipted) and the BRINGING-OUT (12:51 — hotzi YHWH, the book's title-deed, al-tzivotam receipting exo_06's joint charge by authored link). CROSS-UNIT RECEIPTS this unit pays: the send-demand of 5:2 GRANTED at 12:31 (qumu tzeu... KE-DABERKHEM — settlement link authored); the face-ban's ISSUER-BREACH lands (Pharaoh summons Moses by night); 11:8's servants-bow pays with the honor-swap (the king himself); 10:26's not-a-hoof answered in full (12:32, the dead card open by class, the content surrendered); the ask-command of 11:2 performed (12:35, link authored) with 3:22's exact triple (silver, gold, garments); 3:21's favor-ledger closes (12:36) and Gen 15:14's GREAT-PROPERTY clause is PAID (va-yenatzlu — 3:22's verb verbatim); the drive-verb performed (12:39 ki gorshu — 6:1 and 11:1 closed); exo_06's mi-qotzer=430 cipher pays at 12:41 (mi-qetz + the number); the very-day formula runs statute→hosts→event (12:17, 12:41, 12:51); the Jehu-wire lands (12:12, judgments on the gods). Queue at close: FOUR perpetual-opens (calendar, feast, week, answer-script) — plus the standing opens behind (the dead-by-refusal four; the judgment-plea; the bones-oath armed at 13:19). REGISTRY: erev_rav enters at the fold (collective, UNCERTAIN). TESTS 0. CARE-POINTS: the statute's tense (12:17 — the exodus legislated in the perfect before the event); the mashchit's two tracks (12:12 I-alone vs 12:23 the barred destroyer — dual-filed, never merged); the avoda-inversion (12:25 — slavery's word become worship's); the ger's double motion (bound 12:19, admitted 12:48, leveled 12:49 — tora achat); the great-cry file closes (Gen 27:34 → 11:6 → 12:30, asserted); the chazaq-inversion (12:33 — the hardening-verb drives the exodus); the two no-etnachta miniatures (12:45, 12:47). WATCHLIST ARMS: 13:6 (the lean matzot sign's third station, next unit), 13:8 (the telling), 13:19 (the bones), Num 9:11-12 (the second pesach: yokhluhu + the unbroken bone), 29:39 (ben ha-arbayim's daily clock), Lev 23:39 (the chag-file), 32:1 (the erev rav's account opens).
ORAL AUDIT — crowns (chain-attested + DB-verified): ORAL AUDIT 2026-08-09 (IN-PIPELINE, forward era; manifest logic/oral_audit/manifests/exo_12_passover_and_exodus_claims.json 16/16 VERIFIED, zero FAILED; record logic/oral_audit/AUDIT_exo_12_2026-08-09.md). CROWNS (chain-attested + DB-verified). THE MEZUZOT FILE [EX12-01, MS on 12:7] — THE UNIT'S MARQUEE: המזוזת ("the doorposts") exactly THREE in Exodus, all this parasha (12:7 command, 12:22 deed, 12:23 pass-over; census exact), Deut 11:20 full-of-full — and at the SHEMA (Deut 6:9) a genuine STREAM-VS-MASORAH DISCREPANCY: MS's Masorah lean-first (מזזות, the Gemara/Rashi skeleton), the stream full-first (מזוזת) — dual-tracked, with MS's note itself the history of the quarrel. THE ONE FULL EATING [EX12-02, MS on 12:15 + the Ramah]: every eating-word אכל lean in the Torah except ONE full — הלחם אשר הוא אוכל ("the bread which he ate," Gen 39:6, Joseph's master) — verified lemma-whole; beside the one full going (Gen 37:25): the Torah's two one-full words, both in Joseph's Egypt. THE TWO LEAN FIRSTS [EX12-03, MS]: הראשן (12:15) and בראשן (12:18) — the Torah's only lean first-words, BOTH this chapter; the Kitzur's six-hours law from the missing vav. THE FOUR LEAN MATZOT [EX12-04, MS+KB on 12:18]: מצת exactly four (12:18, 13:6, 29:2, Lev 2:4 — census exact, the sign transmitted here); the Zohar's lean at 12:15 vs the stream's full — dual-tracked. THE LEAN LEFTOVER [EX12-05, MS on 12:10]: והנתר lean only here; the whole notar-class full elsewhere — the leftover spelled thinnest where leftovers burn. THE VOKHAKHA STRESS-TRIAL [EX12-06, MS+KB on 12:11]: וככה Torah-unique; Nehemiah's pair; Radak's end-stress vs Provencali's front-stress with the old books' DOUBLE ZARQA — the stream's zarqa checked, dual-tracked. THE GIRDED UNIQUE [EX12-07, MS+KB]: חגרים once in the Torah; Recanati corrected (authority six); Micah's idol crossing the sea and Daniel's angel-girded man named. THE UNHURRIED FUTURE [EX12-08, KB]: בחפזון pair exact (12:11 + Deut 16:3); Isaiah's "NOT in haste" named — the last redemption slower than the first. THE SECOND-PESACH EAT-FILE [EX12-09, KB on 12:8]: יאכלהו exactly three in the Torah (12:8, Lev 8:31, Num 9:11); the eat-framed verse; the miluim stay-inside wire; the harvest-eaters of the last redemption named. THE TRIPLED VERSE [EX12-10, KB on 12:2]: three month-words spaced three apart in the calendar-command — the verse whose syntax is a calendar. THE ORDER-SWAP [EX12-11, KB on 12:22]: doorposts-first at the command, lintel-first at the deed — "changed order still fulfills"; המשקוף census exactly the chapter's three. THE NO-FRANKINCENSE THREE [EX12-12, KB on 12:23]: ולא יתן exactly three (the barred destroyer, Lev 5:11, Num 5:15) — the offerings' merit and the righteous women's. THE LOT PAIR [EX12-13, KB+MS on 12:31]: קומו צאו exactly twice (12:31 + Gen 19:14) — the exodus in Sodom's exit-formula; the tzadi of צאו DAGESHED (one of the five at Daniel 5). THE SUCCOTH PAIR [EX12-14, KB+MS on 12:37]: סכתה exactly twice (12:37 + Gen 33:17) — "by Jacob's merit they went out"; the Rameses vowels ruled. THE DOUBLE WATCHING [EX12-15, KB+MS on 12:42]: שמרים twice in one verse — half for Egypt's exodus, half for the redemption to come; ליל full-yod vs the Meiri (authority seven). THE LETTER-FILE [EX12-16, MS]: the pe of בנגפו ("when He struck") dageshed (checked); the feast-gimels hard; the soft kaf/pe stations; משכו ("draw out") with MS's essay-promise; ועצם ("and a bone") unbound; הבתים two-accent station; the printer's false break at 12:25; מבשל ("boiled") unique with Eli's sons named; the עגת ("cakes of") file; the four Torah-unique night-words — חוצה ("outside"), למהר ("to hasten"), צררת ("bound up"), בחצי ("at the half of") — with their Nakh twins; ואז ("and then") with Lev 26:41; and the gematria quartet (olive's-worth 488, the mila 90, the obligation 21, the covenant-blood 139) asserted at the build.

STEP_Ex_12_6 · Exod.12.6 · BETWEEN_THE_EVENINGS
